Example 21 - ARC latent reasoning with pp-prop

Claim boundary: Example 21 instantiates the paper's public ARC task, ranked-candidate, and variable-effort contract with BrainPy LIF neurons, BrainTrace sparse synapses, and pp-prop. The paper's private data, model dimensions, internal update rules, and training recipe were unavailable. This is not a reproduction, makes no paper-score or inference-cost claim, and asserts no agreement between pp-prop and a BPTT gradient oracle.
